<td id="b-f090eb867f224b819d052c4acbb8a1d3" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-13c9a7a5fa19456b9d8faf6b49d255a5"><span style="font-size:12px">涉政非负面</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-6d15a0d44d21411985ac12f6995d341c" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-eb578facb4744e2980f4718717f1d41f"><span style="font-size:12px"><u>国家领导人正面</u></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-fc59c2d285204b978f896d42728d1f39" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-7b7824c6740d4f6b90aceb8c5dd57714"><span style="font-size:12px">-</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-82465f7faedf4ddf9c6e55bdfaeac4e3" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-6a4c4af958cb452d884c5ab9c0c65612"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-b88e6a08909246d984d64cda47903e92" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-068e9f22206e494abf530b628957ead6"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>内容无负面元素前提下，</blockquote></span></p>
<p id="b-417cc1579cbd4ee79dc040edf11116f0"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>发布已故领导人历史履历</blockquote></span><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote><blockquote>，近期动向，</blockquote></blockquote></span><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>个人任免情况、事迹分享，新闻报道，不区分是否为影视剧内容需管控。</blockquote></span></p>
<p id="b-202b859b0eb34e0ea6b5ad3c9b1d8754"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>提示：涉及负面需按初审标准打击。</blockquote></span></p>
<p id="b-9f3cfb4a29cb49bdae56474cd197b1fb"><span style="font-size:12px">（军政史标准中，提及领导人及初审打，复审兜底）</span></p>
<details id="b-a6ed4325d23945749111ecaafd77e803">
<summary><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>涉政非负面标准（军政史）优先参考附带文档</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<table id="b-77a2315b5fb54045b83768571ebc2272">
<tr id="b-bcb642fdfd5b4401a3b6be56b106c40f">
<th id="b-665f897b3ff64c62901700dc1e520043">
<p id="b-84e1282fad87444d81958f3e66b0b574"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>标签</strong></span></p>
</th>
<th id="b-9302b45cefea4767a35fd92c6626e403">
<p id="b-1fee5805d3f74960b8046ec9c6bc5af6"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>违规点</strong></span></p>
</th>
<th id="b-9f4199f454d2472c9ec66988a8063a19">
<p id="b-bd3b409953384274862eb3901d86ff73"><span style="font-size:12px">风险程度</span></p>
</th>
<th id="b-cdb6a6b8eeb74880ba3a734dca64b2fa">
<p id="b-36c75a0397b6408886108c34ebd3e1dc"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>管控细则</strong></span></p>
</th>
<th id="b-d0888c96f4144534aa51025ab487a0b2">
<p id="b-a7f787535fa44a859e42baddfd27297b"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>初审案例case</strong></span></p>
</th>
<th id="b-7c740a30b4d640e78bc6806fd328527a">
<p id="b-b33c236adeeb4bd29a438956c4ccdd28"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>复审案例case</strong></span></p>
</th>
</tr>
<tr id="b-4e8d2b65788840cc9d26b5d6ed0d0c79">
<td id="b-4bb75aecc6784d148667820d9e92a1f1">
<p id="b-9cfd37b251f541b2a73303a3dbad3700"><span style="font-size:12px">一号非负面</span></p>
<p id="b-4bbbc5bd86a04f7baf2f1920da2cdf0c"></p>
<p id="b-65106b0fed70447f85098b2a5951a34d"><span style="font-size:12px">国家领导人非负面</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8f53020afd2349c79399737939a88edf">
<p id="b-ac32eea81dc74149bc0fe5a8c030cfb6" style="text-align:center"><span style="font-size:12px">涉政人物非负面</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-70332ecc56204903894c32e4a1ef79dc">
<p id="b-c6454f28ed1e4a21ab4de62d1e6155eb"><span style="font-size:12px">高</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-3bba20d6b772411f9f3ce7e628452d85">
<p id="b-7f8daa54ce4a42159570f6c4f3175b00"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>初审：</strong></span></span></p>
<p id="b-91929c761a2c4b3dbab02a73180558c7"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>原则：线上内容出现即打，一律从严；线下内容非主体通过。</strong></span></span></p>
<p id="b-75e255b981d84fd4bc4953a53ce2fd7f"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">1）内容中出现</span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>国内一号位、二号位，包含且不限于核心领导人（现任七常委）</strong></span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">、涉现任一号、历届一号以及提及相关直系亲属的内容，出现一帧即打。如无法识别领导人，根据视频内容判断，提及近现代领导人的即打。</span></span></p>
<ul id="b-0428cb3eae6f4fb49efbc5dd2ccbec14">
<li id="b-ecfa9fb764e44b0ab032aab4f01073e7"><span style="font-size:12px">开国至今的领导班子、共产党、国民党派的所有人物。（开国著名人物包括：彭德怀，陈独秀等我党开国领导人，蒋介石，张学良等国民党著名人物。）</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-4705ce1bc53a4102a782b96dc316ee6f">
<li id="b-e30d0a96bafb40eca961f509a23d6e11"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">重要领导人出访活动：</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">领导视察工作，如习近平问候乡镇企业。</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-92f06833986240af92a3fa73c47d7425">
<li id="b-0f60831c58764bb9b4432099320c0e36"><span style="font-size:12px">缅怀/悼念性质国家已故领导人的内容。（人物范围都要打）</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-fcc3691863ec4ec4971ccfa2e17b1632">
<li id="b-e0f8336257bb40fd9f5b1c0ff691e53f"><span style="font-size:12px">过度个人崇拜、神话领导人，或以封建帝制称呼领导人，如“毛太祖、毛太宗、毛主席万岁、习帝”</span></li>
<li id="b-a68a83dca3e3462f87fdbbfb928eba9e"><span style="font-size:12px">引用领导人讲话，不区分视频内容or文案部分。（只打标有署名的名言）</span></li>
<li id="b-a6af3b648d0b49f4a094e80242c1703b"><span style="font-size:12px">模仿领导人、对比领导人，无论正负向。</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-064d1838b0b942ffa20ada777a6780c8">
<li id="b-64b6ec4fc0b44bad9e5edbe4dc6d350e"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">单纯发布已故领导人的重要正面讲话、语录，如毛泽东等领导人的诗词内容。（同样有署名的才打标。）</span></span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-185b2b6989cd472cbcf405dd6f06d0e5">
<li id="b-6dad6d8bf2df4421a0c13f1930bf824e"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">线下的博物馆、纪念馆、官方宣传展览，</span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><u>只打视频主体</u></span></span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-f8a0f19b694646c18df79e9dcd87ad39"><span style="font-size:12px">2）</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>国外领导人</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">现任或历任的传统最高领袖（国王、女王、天皇等国家元首），现代国家领袖（总统、总理、首相、国家主席等），如拜登、特朗普、奥巴马、布什、克林顿....里希、伊丽莎白及其直系亲属、叙利亚总统及夫人等。（任何国家在任一号，近现代历届著名一号。）</span></p>
<p id="b-2900281227d44001b7ecc2eee3219212"><span style="font-size:12px">无法辨识情况下只要视频中提到某国领导人即打标。</span></p>
<p id="b-dad6b144258843f4aa8378a3d3ac0d89"></p>
<p id="b-b8721fc47d3649059b14369c025aba6e"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>复审：</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-f5dca977f83c417ca539795a6529c7b4"><span style="font-size:12px">加强力度兜底。</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-5030abc76ed94c049776b99c44f87d9f">
<p id="b-e8342468ae3342ab9bfab6b27dcbe37c"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>重要领导人出访活动：</strong></span></span></p>
<p id="b-c0e7fc998653480bafb4563a6240566a"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98061597702?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="119" height="154" id="b-e116daa95c99431c92f7516bb77af7f9" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98059425142?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="196" height="164" id="b-01accd1718944bd3963358370b246c64" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-d25764b62c494c0d9e8f1d78bc5d0e49"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>缅怀/悼念性质国家已故领导人</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-61a709d544644e2ea0fecb3ce4e022ff"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98058786047?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="146" height="166" id="b-600ee2b6fa6640de8cbdbe50ebcc0012" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98058198907?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="108" height="153" id="b-9385472b30a842a8b85ef60d1e173d34" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98057719943?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="109" height="159" id="b-9a5660ecc3cb42c288a6e5ad96c32821" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-1375d60150504148b74a16a57ec176dc"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>引用领导人讲话</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-50753a375ec64571b5f4150801845eaa"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98060223745?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="132" height="187" id="b-9602f77f7680446b8668e637fa3f0f1a" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98060223743?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="129" height="194" id="b-a3014401eaa14c23a1e26328f9c0fd12" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98055436043?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="226" height="96" id="b-5b8c34d0ebfb49d1b267a0bb4a354da3" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-e5cd6a216f764c71b6cff4613a564401"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>官方公映过的正向纪录片（里面出现领导人就打）</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-aa0a035bf09a48f3ac3f11d88d2452ab"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98055436035?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="228" height="104" id="b-299f79e3912949a8b4f49fe24a092616" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-4d1c3babd4f54ac185377acbe0a5068f"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>国外领导人</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-d9e79875fd61442ab8f01cc0ae2cd5f0"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98059264524?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="177" height="127" id="b-a89768b6a6b74935a9b335b1104dc2d5" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98056071914?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="237" height="127" id="b-07c73ef1de534b449063cb080a3282b0" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98057560872?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="94" height="119" id="b-b487fbf8f60649128b55d583ad1c90a6" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c8d061b5f2d64eea9c1eadd51b14f228">
<p id="b-2950c09337384f51942df4c9452a67d8"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-59e57b90d30a491587b1ed12862b5c34">
<td id="b-537f2382bd854b06bcda1b45699bd41c">
<p id="b-f454112a1a27457c9fc9ede92653a7e9"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>敏感事件</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-3dd41c6250d64aa0b2c23f06239b2880"></p>
<p id="b-a309f4c3d0924906af00d6060d2be86f"><span style="font-size:12px">国家形象非负面</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-f0fe15d3281344a7aa9637d41403244d">
<p id="b-fb39e874ddf74d6ba092cf41ee93ff93"><span style="font-size:12px">党军政类</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c33273a2d31e4f17b7e5dae5483acfdf">
<p id="b-6bc8f38255984d4a976f11d6e0cd0259"><span style="font-size:12px">高</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-96917ed1bdbc4a3d9eaa680e14a0fcf4">
<p id="b-3116414c8e9b45e49edebda164797621"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>初审：</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-0e8bc19dcd164662ad4bc9c76d9da287"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>出现即打，无需区分主体</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-afcbdf0115b74233a787113e768948b4"><span style="font-size:12px">涉及</span><span style="font-size:12px"><u>发布/转载</u></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">政治、外交、军事（包括武器展示）、民族、国家安全、宗教、治安（重大社会安全事件）、经济、犯罪（包括许家印案件）</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">为主体或者评论内容，不区分国内或国外，现代或历史，也不区分表现形式，均需处理</span></p>
<p id="b-83e8ee086b4d4439ada28ce892eede38"><span style="font-size:12px"><u>视频表现形式包括科普，纪录片，评述等，都要打标。影视作品见下面标准。</u></span></p>
<p id="b-896ca185f4564aa7bf949b3f074b1062"><span style="font-size:12px">1）内容涉及党和国家大的政治方向，面向全国全党的时政类政治条例、制度等重大信息披露、揭露，全国性的整治活动、主客场外交活动、国外选举、所有政府工作人员任免的报道、解读。</span></p>
<p id="b-6812d93664cd41d7bd9346cf92839bbd"><span style="font-size:12px">2）政府发布的通知公告、会议活动、政治查处、视察考察、政府培训内容。</span></p>
<p id="b-5023fbd97a0145708809c025821cafae"><span style="font-size:12px">3）涉及军队、警方相关的时政内容，国内军队、军人/警方处罚、任免、建立、身故、通报，无论正负面报道。</span></p>
<p id="b-174f2e68c7624a5297a40756cda9b450"><span style="font-size:12px">4）所有近现代政治相关内容和评述，包括大陆与台湾关系，中外关系，国家政治局势。</span></p>
<p id="b-8323bc0b59e245ca88166a6ecba31c20"><span style="font-size:12px">5）近现代涉民族演变、历史宗教传播等科普内容。</span></p>
<p id="b-f3f6c232bcf14905856cfe7342f133ef"></p>
<p id="b-4786302d454b48789610550388e44caf"><span style="color:rgb(0, 186, 115)"><span style="font-size:12px">不打标：</span></span></p>
<p id="b-e5aae8022f29406d85c4c0199ad343db"><span style="font-size:12px">地震灾害等非时政类社会事件的正向报道。</span></p>
<p id="b-e4bbbaf6d1574bf7ba47e08d5424d023"><span style="font-size:12px">正向：主旨突出企业捐助赈灾，志愿者搜救队救灾等。</span></p>
<p id="b-efe26cd563b64ebd95aeb25d4d2073b3"><span style="font-size:12px">负面：主旨突出地震死了****人，建筑损毁损失****元。</span></p>
<p id="b-9674f204cda544d8920f36387b1f5589"></p>
<p id="b-2119a90827d2488c9dcd6fa7c2a45aa0"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>复审：</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-78a4b25dff274d79b530f5020cc85517"><span style="font-size:12px">加强力度兜底。</span></p>
<p id="b-cfd30767bd964a9eaa17c99665c9076a"></p>
<p id="b-a71a32bd2d844cd7a6e7b25e7275dfbb"><span style="font-size:12px">豁免非主体：社会案件，出现警察执勤的镜头。</span></p>
<p id="b-3fe98bc75fe640249802168181ff9ed0"><span style="font-size:12px">1）社会事件和案件判断标准为舆情和指令没有提及。</span></p>
<p id="b-04bc35d961404597bbe6377603e0e7a9"><span style="font-size:12px">2）如果涉及相关事件/案件非视频主体场景。</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-a4079652544548e99bcb25e8288ad85e">
<p id="b-7797c49494f64626b8101b1de15fa8dd"><span style="font-size:12px">1）</span><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98061756949?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="127" height="185" id="b-f02f17422d9644b38a3c8c4b727de8a2" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98058786045?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="117" height="175" id="b-49d8b7137deb49b99b38a096b9d60fb9" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98056071909?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="313" height="163" id="b-9eb571b85e494eb4b0dca97dd14dfb87" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-7d0e10ce2eba4fb8b2b21de9dac5dbe1"><span style="font-size:12px">2）</span><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98062632195?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="187" height="99" id="b-7b89daaf1f3644a6b287a89260602256" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98061020223?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="133" height="199" id="b-ebcf3bfa48ad4469bebb9d7229810aae" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-ea7fd535bb504a1893a1af85f2ceb98e"><span style="font-size:12px">3)</span><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98062712597?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="138" height="225" id="b-380e1af4a3f148739064a9c9a436a596" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98057879206?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="145" height="215" id="b-306afbbea222477eb7138bf83dc20c7a" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-dc213dd5dcaf40989a13d5236a00e2d7"><span style="font-size:12px">4)</span><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98056071905?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="130" height="178" id="b-e0e91b26eb9d4a4c82a8c4ee17a9da51" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98057217574?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="316" height="144" id="b-9ee1e0cc235d4978bfaf67ec693290dd" alt="image.jpeg"></p>

<tr id="b-6c37a3fb617940588e48dfd216788981">
<td id="b-6a0e5b7b1a4a480c836e78b4cc58cf23">
<p id="b-4da31cdb46a344ac875696f677e292de"><span style="font-size:12px">历史内容</span></p>
<p id="b-fb16114cba1a45c6a8aa645020e97907"></p>
<p id="b-2df6b9e1ee6145faa5e8e26fa44f986d"><span style="font-size:12px">国家形象非负面</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-b30b73e01f7d429c9e2563e17cb8e3d8">
<p id="b-50aa051225dc4176821cd6d13b93c1c6"><span style="font-size:12px">涉史事件/人物</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-bf47552f85d443ce8558163efc8f24a3">
<p id="b-570f20e992c44b06b8e60e3466b97f3d"><span style="font-size:12px">高</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-1d8a123bb33a4e7886ed3b9e48eee099">
<p id="b-e9fa802566e34623a2de15d7624a0921"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>初审：需区分主体</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-e4b2a263970b447aae6f5b9912faae87"><span style="font-size:12px">主要打击中国近代史、现代史相关人物及国家事件的科普，正向古代史内容不打压。</span></p>
<p id="b-8ad19611e2fd4464aa947a0511314b8c"><span style="font-size:12px">打标：</span></p>
<ul id="b-97e7c39decb94b869c2e54b70287d30f">
<li id="b-93850909a9d7420ea5c5f30400052da9"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>涉史事件</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>讲述重大历史事件及战役事件</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">，重点讲述历史战争事件，包括但不限于鸦片战争、太平天国运动、北京条约、洋务运动、中法战争、甲午中日战争、马关条约、戊戌变法、义和团运动、八国联军侵华战争、辛丑条约、辛亥革命、预备立宪、南昌起义、八七会议、秋收起义、九一八时间、红军长征、遵义会议、华北事变、西安事变、卢沟桥事变、南京大屠杀、百团大战、国民党大战、辽沈战役、淮海战役、平津战役等重大战役及历史大事件的科普、剖析、深度讲解。</span>.</li>
<li id="b-49bbe3882730406b9016006936fb9df1"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>涉史革命人物</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：主旨讲述参与过重大历史战争、事件，或与战争事件相关的历史人物、抗战英雄、</span><span style="color:rgb(212, 30, 33)"><span style="font-size:12px">革命家</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">、间谍、特务等的</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>抗战经历</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">，不区分正负向，包括但不限于文革时期林彪、朱德、纪登奎、汪东兴、陈锡联、吴德等敏感人物；以及</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>调侃历史人物的花边感情史，批判其人为作风等带有讽刺导向的内容。</strong></span>.</li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-0c9462f0eba7426ebb6586ae7f4160f2">
<li id="b-eaf95d01492c4277b5abc682c0bb1c30">
<p id="b-3c7aeaa7aa384c368939c5649216952c"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>国外历史及战争及革命事件</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：正向讲述如文艺复兴、美国独立战争、法国大革命、日本明治维新等国外历史事件，如果涉及屠杀、战争等不适场景按照“引人不适”打压，解说</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>影视剧、混剪、拆条放在下面</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">。</span>.</p>
<p id="b-70a777660784433ab81e9c080bed2172"></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p id="b-8cb40da1ddf146dbb2efbcc933f80242"></p>
<p id="b-85c18b4e0b304ce8a05a926782f15f14"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>复审</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：</span></p>
<p id="b-2d77d4122ee44f0abd9e00a086ea5375"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">打标：</span></span></p>
<ul id="b-814b24206d634a7f90e054284a1474be">
<li id="b-5bd6cc566c304af18fe25f85b9a32f6c"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>正向讲述、科普，</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">国内近代历史中参与过历史战争事件、或与历史事件相关、有舆论的</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>文学家、建筑家、科学家、艺术家、知识分子</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">等的真实人物生平、履历、感情史等与</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>非主旨讲述抗战</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">的内容。. 如鲁迅、周作人、胡适、梁思成、林徽因、郁达夫、老舍、朱自清、闻一多等，如讲述梁思成和林徽因的爱情故事，鲁迅的诗句等，内容中可能会提及XX参与过五四运动、XX是民主战士等。</span>.</li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-c935b255b4a24190bc53f4ce52342be2">
<li id="b-cba66d42ec6a4d21b44c50e26d76e655"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>正向科普及负面调侃讽刺，批评，评价</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">国内历史事件、或历史人物。.（古代史）</span></li>
<li id="b-ca7102cb9a83459298e60b2329f0919e"><span style="font-size:12px">讲述、科普</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>中国古代史</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">的内容，相关历史事件、风俗人文、社会风貌、历史人物等科普。</span>.</li>
<li id="b-9a387cbe5bd34a5baaa459947311e70b"><span style="font-size:12px">科普</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>国内外历史年表、历史场景复原</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">，如不同国家100年前对比。</span>.</li>
</ul>
<p id="b-fd61c013803b4030a4138470a8644e22"></p>
<aside id="b-1125ce5b26164febb6c3f24422ea2987" class="note">
<strong></strong>
<div>
<p id="b-74443ff940884d848c2d332c066e9690"><strong><a href="https://baijiahao.baidu.com/s?id=1767232731589714094&amp;wfr=spider&amp;for=pc" title="中国近代史大事年表" id="b-e06f02cf199341c0bff05f7a4f0d6923">中国近代史大事年表</a></strong><strong>（时间轴及重大事件参考）</strong></p>
<ul id="b-9472865773ad4739be902ef4e0b93db4">
<li id="b-b2762ed842ea437b8cd90c33d4245ab9">中国古代史：晚清鸦片战争爆发前，即1840年之前，包含原始社会、奴隶社会（约公元前2070年至公元前476年）、封建社会（公元前475年至1840年）</li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-5fce538abb1c4add853cf9d67b79b800">
<li id="b-22dd0caa347340d8a777907d840662e2">中国近代史：1840-1949年，按朝代划分可分为两部分：晚清时期（1840-1911）侵略与抗争；中华民国时期（1912-1949）</li>
<li id="b-ff01eb6dae964d2da49968a000fa57e5">中国现代史：1949年至今</li>
</ul>
</div>
</aside>
</td>
<td id="b-50621ff5b8a644479f27bbd5605cbabe">
<ul id="b-cd76fcec76fe406f97673e4e8c6c23a9">

</ul>

<tr id="b-20952808f35d40cba2b0525dd604a758">
<td id="b-46a2f370ba27494282cecd3c9b4ea61a">
<p id="b-d4ed7be373524927b6577f087968e4a8"><span style="font-size:12px">国家形象非负面</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-fc30060e8d11499785b73f6cef880851">
<p id="b-284cedd8d7bc49c1b29591dba73cdbe2"><span style="font-size:12px">军事战争</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-feca60dee15847e7973d5c58ab4ad984">
<p id="b-c78198aea1034ee483c57f5e8cb3d51b"><span style="font-size:12px">高</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-b3356b936e094d18b160c2d3d6509e90">
<p id="b-c323fdd09bf14caf8f8d8bec6cd27fa7"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>初审</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>把控主体。</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-bdc2a2c487d941309da18d05a96127f6"><span style="font-size:12px">军事战争纪实、军事战争讲解、军事武器科普的内容出现即打。纪录片都打</span></p>
<ul id="b-419379646ec745ca86aae55f77ff9de7">
<li id="b-6cd83bbd87684f74a65f4cd0a1abe763"><span style="font-size:12px">讲述、分析、评判国内及国际上发生过及正在发生的</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>军事战争</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">、国家军事战争，军事演练、军队讲解、军队战力对比、国际军事局势；</span></li>
<li id="b-f8cc392afbc141d28dc2531e51a7da61">
<p id="b-6523f8605739409591d07aecd4706de3"><span style="font-size:12px">主旨围绕军事战争讲解、科普</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>军事武器（不豁免动画版）</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-94671f7df56341f59219637f4b3ec2cf"></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p id="b-6c8b90d7bca243ad898404f56d568a4b"></p>
<p id="b-8970994bb02f4fef8f955e8ad186d877"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>复审：</strong></span></p>
<ul id="b-19941b50d04b4013a089e9486bac1d49">
<li id="b-32fbc10b90284774b639185a170ba2b8"><span style="font-size:12px">主旨讲述、拍摄战争中或战争后的非暴力场景，如实地探访、讲述战后国家的生活实况、人文，主要目的是为了展现人间真情、国家情谊、主旨传递的正能量内容，如叙利亚的灾后重建生活，儿童生活现状的偏旅游向、人文向内容。</span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-5b40d3705c0345fdaf742869059e4c82"></p>
<p id="b-f7cd57ebbcfa47eba8034bc571e99abe"><span style="font-size:12px">不打标</span></p>
<ul id="b-9bfe8affc14440899fd0373feb2e178b">
<li id="b-941159235d2d4b6cb2b8610f868d03bd">
<p id="b-a0f4f4990dc44d519849d2f1c26dcc4d"><span style="font-size:12px">非重大军事战争使用的重大的武器科普，如靶场枪械、军刀、冷兵器科普等。</span></p>
<p id="b-c252675ec52340c0b32ab6f599fbd4ae"></p>
<p id="b-95963c0a84f445e9ba9fb065e7469ee9"></p>
</li>
</ul>
</td>
<td id="b-fb3a0995be0a4de8a6d3b91a10e2d3b4">
<p id="b-1224696865c541639c2e61612079fc68"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>军事战争资讯、讲解、军事演练</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-8b84c1bb0bfa4d50ae681458222590f3"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98057376631?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="228" height="303" id="b-05730253021d464aa95b0196cc50b70c" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98060223657?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="218" height="305" id="b-a4c49db81dc1468d8d0949b2c0434132" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98062236364?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="291" height="164" id="b-d7e2643d0510478abe1eaf3134373337" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-9018a0b167f14df594d30063a5b287cc"></p>
<p id="b-d464956d302d492abe0b3bf26e50f8ab"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>国际战争专业分析</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-45f57eaa01724d44996a61c920d5c7a5"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98058946115?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="311" height="169" id="b-8d608912e7c44f9eaad029a2669d9244" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-af8c5b7ea6254406a17c8c056685804e"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>军事重型武器科普：</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-ef0e855e40564bc7954c555dad02562b"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98060702086?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="314" height="181" id="b-b8aa2d69f2a747629c4d16df5be64c82" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-0034442bdd034cc7bf78a1edbc20cb54"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>动画版军事武器科普</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-db1d84d6cbd44a1f82bb5f399f4ee8f6"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98055596470?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="310" height="174" id="b-150b993634eb4b269302c60532f8fb85" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-5316b922e9a14d5fab2dce9810284089">
<p id="b-0ab4f2fb64864d99b2918797a993b662"><span style="font-size:12px">叙利亚战后实地探访、人文、社会实况，（无血腥、残忍暴力、伤亡的场景），主要为展现国家友谊，传递国际间正能量</span></p>
<p id="b-ccf2ab7116bc4f469ea8b46de761a33f"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98062076127?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="175" height="291" id="b-e77b675ea8124b05aba98559d54dcc54" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98059744227?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="177" height="322" id="b-0069677e8e504d99a8bbcb1d186be1c6" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98060702087?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="165" height="304" id="b-7932f4a506324aba852ae19d60a10e1c" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98059106487?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="248" height="157" id="b-f0c833241e3d45f08d479eee2de9e03a" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-ae514718cb9f4846b8e103a51c3ee244"></p>
<p id="b-e3557863e6b444c99af405aa03d650b6"></p>
<p id="b-0760798b37b84311a17fd4daacd470ef"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>影视剧内容</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-8b04c863c8cd492684d845c63b7b991d"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98061597617?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="287" height="154" id="b-8763b9f7a716409191ab53832525ad2b" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-1bdfa24eb5354eaab75bcdd648066a69">
<td id="b-e460a0d0dde04e9c948ab1ef120f713b">
<p id="b-49884dbda3fc4fed9f5b2f7e932d5b38"><span style="font-size:12px">历史内容</span></p>
<p id="b-cde6528a856f4ed7b6722b561dd83139"><span style="font-size:12px">国家形象非负面</span></p>
<p id="b-054f7c12cbea431eb8d4fe3ce8a733e1"></p>
<p id="b-46efe9bb4b6e4b6f85fc23ee9122f358"></p>
<p id="b-05cc782c5fc64cf0b299f6e44fc07308"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-5803a420f9a64eaa9845da95f8ea8551">
<p id="b-0ffbf0ca8c0d4d6092500e1fe07e1175"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>敏感影视剧</strong></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c5ce19f30ab74f438dbc4cd8a0231216">
<p id="b-fcebca9bf159499f8a1fe2c3f9ba689f"><span style="font-size:12px">中</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-d992b0f92f0d417f89dc9f1c206fefa4">
<p id="b-d0de35d332f64e6581178c745a2cba04"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>初审</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：打压可能涉及</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>虚假或负向评价</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">历史人物、历史事件的古代史、近代史、现代的影视内容。</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>如果涉及以上“涉政、涉史、涉军”的科普内容，优先打高风险标签</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">。</span></p>
<ul id="b-5c530b4a953c44539c7cc88fd6e98005">
<li id="b-7a9db1dee0e54cccb3bb40054cc2eb6d"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>视频标题带有疑问，反问，标题党（标题带有虚假或者夸张成分）的形式解说近现代或者历史人物的影视剧，</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">如“雍正娶了100个妻子”</span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-2d505c7d509e488eb304e53bd0d585aa"></p>
<p id="b-80fc72a866ff4f50a41b597448a157a5"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>复审</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：打压</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>评论区可能存在风险</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">的真实近代史题材的影视剧解说。</span></p>
<ul id="b-2478c2bee6dd42b79d9b8cfac026a9ed">
<li id="b-b31dbdc468e4476fb09ef4011ba03e4c"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>讲解中国近代史及现代史</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">的如建国相关、抗战相关的影视剧内容，如《走向共和》，《觉醒年代》、《亮剑》，</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>不区分正负向</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">，抗战相关题材影视片就打。</span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-890ed5a0a8be40059cdcfa8306e56464"></p>
<p id="b-d963f811ed9d44eeacb9aa8ae8f14dee"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>豁免</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">：</span></p>
<ul id="b-150af0bdc71143eb98d5c40f503f847d">
<li id="b-f83af796d4bf421c9358d4ab5d779c39"><span style="font-size:12px">客观介绍</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>古代史</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">相关的影视剧剪辑、混剪、盘点、拆条、剧情讲解。 </span></li>
<li id="b-9786e9601d1d4a79a711e768fadc8e59"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>非真实历史的古装剧讲解</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">、混剪。</span></li>
<li id="b-6aa67e357b0343f18b372bcd4eeee4bf"><span style="font-size:12px">军事题材影视剧解说、混剪，如《战狼》、《红海行动》等。 </span></li>
<li id="b-6d2c51905f47473fa0fbabe20e18fa7f"><span style="font-size:12px">1978年改革开放期间的百姓生活题材电视剧混剪、解说。如《大江大河》，《山海情》、《青岛往事》，且主旨不是讲述战争的内容。</span></li>
</ul>
</td>
<td id="b-d1e5019c64d54da9a966eeb236fd7516">
<p id="b-d68891320c104fca88cd8e0030a14f3a"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>标题党类古代史影视剧解说</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-52f7cc664e0d4c688b8a703b6c04cab8"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98060223655?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="177" height="281" id="b-0b82f3f4426c41b1a1c9fd54a68f221a" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-06df537386fb49aaaf44afa628e93671">
<p id="b-dc96566dcf9b403d9662bcb06d74126b"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>中国近代史相关的影视内容</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-0cc5970ae5ad4b24bcac3c9a16370358"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98058625706?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="199" height="125" id="b-d827739b6e51415f9608e452b18cf92a" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-bfbb32b7da6049e8bae1175d69b879c6"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98057217495?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="182" height="106" id="b-4a110d60d1a14959b9b21c9e3ac700d8" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98062236353?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="196" height="114" id="b-20a9684b32894b939c35de6db5fb012d" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98062553206?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="209" height="127" id="b-d2c55858eb074a5b8040fbc09ec6089e" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-d4771489109d46eab198b16d4f381eea"></p>
<p id="b-66d7c1b41dda41b787d071c67c6057d6"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>豁免：改革开放百姓生活影视剧</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-118394f447f8467e933fe40adcb6d739"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98059264433?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="255" height="147" id="b-eee5ff9f802e433092df93372837eedc" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/98061020130?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="242" height="138" id="b-0e5478aaba014d0089c993074bf38e40" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>

<td id="b-4a88e0b13c1943a585313ec661b59e20">

<p id="b-da4f08f1c28b43039baf9f2a7dceb5c9"><span style="font-size:12px">涉政非负面</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e235cd8e27024605a143956fca90c106" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-6dbbd7035abf488197f7406b3cbd7034"><span style="font-size:12px"><u>战争近代史</u></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e50cd1506991421987da1dc675122d93" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-aad8352ad4504224857e4ce98fb4f6ae"><span style="font-size:12px">-</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-9be931f848c04b4ea0a741acc7200eb0" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-da2e0707969d4e959bbd7abab941f66f"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>日本侵华暴行</blockquote></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-ec0275adbf99421a83d8b2a70f4d56bd" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-f925ea07b7e74871996d4ea4c52f1f45"><span style="font-size:12px">视频主旨展示日本侵华战争影片或纪实，且内容展现为残害蹂躏妇女幼童、残杀百姓等暴行需打击。</span></p>
<p id="b-d244417f64ad4c24afad84ba231a77aa"><span style="font-size:12px">（更新在军政史初审标准，为初审打，复审兜底）</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e507cb1ce5b14673852c6b8829b3b2ff" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-d2e8e27187a94135b2c93cd5a31f8017"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/106814152268?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="86" height="113" id="b-bd9193777953400e89a5050240233e11" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/106813036728?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="77" height="139" id="b-8d4dfe63ee8f4f4d88dec4460f0eaf4b" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/106814355321?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="237" height="118" id="b-d6016c934cc648b1bc53f8bf9b2b68d2" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-66c722b1dbf74c50888fd4caf387b963" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-17d3b8a53bd24ff6b5604f98b91c1dcf"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-422f54d29efa4788941356c231b617b6">
<td id="b-17a714487dc8486597bfd8634363edbd">
<p id="b-b392e31697584cd3afe7039f0aa3214e"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-61316003d65c434a88acd708675f11d1" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-ad02536c3d174744a6849f8d4d44f4f9"><span style="font-size:12px">引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-d679cac58220483a851329402ad741a5" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-8b5ebfce3805431e97a584d59b3f9999"><span style="font-size:12px">轻度令人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-6bc91ac78eef4bf284fe9eb78e0ccd3f" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-33dc140ddaf045e69cdf9c19b286b6e0"><span style="font-size:12px">人体轻微引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-6b993664351b4485b081e708ade10004" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-c062d2f024ca48cd85780f73266fcaf4"><span style="font-size:12px">产后肚皮引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-69b9e537ddf64ce4b5c0b24a78cc68c4" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-c4177c4911414a0290c736f06e2565b2"><span style="font-size:12px">视频主旨特写展示产后肚皮松弛下垂、严重妊辰纹等需打击。 主旨展示产后修复可通过</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e88d4b51c18f42b0a247b282f3cc4be0" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-2d62d337eb1a4cc1823fc72d05336b0b"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53185069050?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="54" height="120" id="b-f4d201925beb455282a889ef7db99cda" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53179795485?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="51" height="111" id="b-0932e59cffbd444b9054947b9528de45" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53182681410?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="160" height="118" id="b-27688637d43c4f199eb101c517abf5da" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-391c1a7d60d8484880f7f08ed9f957cc" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-28d24660ba7742728ab6717d437d7f72"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53181072954?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="171" height="112" id="b-ab5d2c388c114a9a809fc98f71f82d15" alt="image.jpeg"></p>

<td id="b-6a6ad7727bc94d108096a9c12e4428e2" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-8366030b91e14b088c47b82a993690cc"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>展示即将进行的以制作食物为目的，对猪、牛、羊、驴、马等大型动物进行伤害、屠宰，展示过程；</blockquote></span></p>

<p id="b-33606d5b8dee47b4bca5a6f9151cfb50"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:12px">主旨展示宰杀后动物头部画面需按照引人不适打击</span></span></p>

<td id="b-9af84bdb90f041c6b559869ed3a9bb3d" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-05d8e70849694eaba8829bc4d7bbb774"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>主旨人物未非洲唇盘族（又叫摩尔西族）的族民，或穿令箭、长脖子等特殊身体特征的人</blockquote></span><span style="font-size:12px">（初审管控，复审兜底）</span></p>
<p id="b-3f4f0667181540039a6d5602073e1eba"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>主旨展示或特写裹小脚</blockquote></span><span style="font-size:12px">（初审管控，复审兜底）</span></p>
<p id="b-36ac565b30ef4d8098772fc90c8e03c5"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>主旨展示或特写人特殊身体特征、畸形等</blockquote></span><span style="font-size:12px">（初审管控，复审兜底）</span></p>
<p id="b-d999d712f1514e7b9c4d35a3dce3e1f4"><span style="font-size:12px">主旨展示或特写残疾人的面部/肢体/智力/精神残疾特征</span></p>
<p id="b-3e6e8a8319b04fd2a0a93dd0bb072cb5"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">非严重视觉畸形残疾人员，例如聋哑人正能量非卖惨类无需打击</span></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-0eee94d4471b4f5db00aa160e81f5e0d" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-f7b064e8476343bc8e17d7226640c6cf"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53172898496?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="126" height="69" id="b-6f337eb1a5a242ea84dd0df1ef37c8aa" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53172898489?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="97" height="72" id="b-a9472078adda4b8ca900ac445ad9d2ac" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53177024827?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="131" height="79" id="b-b2feb8c9343042b68fab5d3309aaf348" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53184278617?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="37" height="85" id="b-dc2debe6f71144ff8ed0d2b7c03db86f" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53184436895?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="37" height="81" id="b-fa9753d8783f41d0a40483a6fdb13525" alt="image.jpeg"></p>

<p id="b-7f43480172034f6f816e75b234bdf63f"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>婴儿胎盘</blockquote></span></p>

<td id="b-2b6530931a8c41799e5c6812d6ca75ba" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<p id="b-20bdb949c32f4dceb202ea443e5c3beb"><span style="font-size:12px">视频内容展示特写婴儿胎盘画面、或线下模拟婴儿在子宫中的状态，并展现逼真的胎盘模型特写需打击。</span></p>
<p id="b-13ba4e3d1be847d6aceb2a27214bde04"><span style="font-size:12px">豁免：视频全部以卡通动画形式科普婴儿在子宫中的状态</span></p>
<p id="b-12e862263c884fa0911ab535a9ad854e"><span style="font-size:12px">（初审管控，复审兜底）</span></p>

</td>
<td id="b-ee9812a5f1f243489f8ff20bc823797f" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<ul id="b-458a27acfe2245e7a40b444c9d6033e0">
<li id="b-e191fd9b81834aeaa732672b0accdc0b"><span style="font-size:12px">视频中出现猎奇特效（通过特效制作的猎奇内容、畸形演绎）目前高爆需打击，后续迁移到三审打击</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-be93a3be24c14458b4f1fa52f6a08a5a">
<li id="b-4bba30cfb02e4af3891d87c3b88e9f08"><span style="font-size:12px">猎奇残忍的风俗展示、科普，如印尼断指祭奠亲人、给死人洗澡、非洲割礼、赤脚走火炭等糟粕文化猎奇、残忍风俗介绍、解说、真实纪录等需打击。不豁免影视剧解说</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-c069d59754144dc1b1c02e5d60dadebc">
<li id="b-c5f85c4997024970942ec6d0d20440d8"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">死刑现场展示、死刑执行等猎奇案件，离奇死亡，猎奇科普，擦边猎奇的纪实片等，出现即控。（仅影</span></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>视综类</strong></span></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">需要以上形式必须为主体展示才打击）【注：如以上涉及强奸案等涉性致死的内容，则按照涉性软色情打击】</span></span></li>
</ul>
</td>

<td id="b-ba0dac8a7fe043feb387e29e8d4e910f" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-7ebeebfc64574e07a7cd4b20fe2c23ec"><span style="font-size:12px">引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-bd742b041cbc44fa88993f3b0250cb0e" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-73fe5243649e4025a5d746b7ce45e9a4"><span style="font-size:12px">轻度令人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-10e21ca43edb4539a5af81c57f0a1dd4" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-e6f1083fea5d494fa94a52a6caaa0c27"><span style="font-size:12px">动物轻微引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-91eb80f7a30a46939d72088d703a821a">
<p id="b-f913d339abf34a1e8be97a3db7e143fa"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>未死透的动物美食类</blockquote></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c0c8783707e34728b110e7c2d6d59c4e">
<p id="b-834e8e8332cd4ab4a535e1f5abfeec55"><span style="font-size:12px">在非正常饮食场合下，展示常理认知中易引人不适的食材原料。易引人不适的实物原材料如：牛蛙（不区分是否去皮）、蝉蛹、黄鳝、泥鳅等。注：如该类实物在正常饮食场合食用无需管控；单纯拍摄该类大量单体原材料，则按照现有标准执行。（初审管控，复审兜底）</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-a1ceb6e8d25b4e6f939c7ae25e725ab8">
<p id="b-bb27b73202f040f8ba0eab85c1f43339"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-ac9de66a3b1a49d7bc8b09d665f03f0a">
<p id="b-2671f15282d04a9097b714b7d13c35ec"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/61926783039?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="59" height="88" id="b-f8abc4acb69e42b0a5c3766211c445ff" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
</tr>

<td id="b-61c14fadb214471287d7e21702729983">
<p id="b-ecf0a48e2fd640bd843dc742d802fa75"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-bd1aa721f60b4db4b89ff73b6b53b908" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-0fd6aeb9029c422fa2a10314f68fb187"><span style="font-size:12px">引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-87b38147ce6b4b3aa7fc9e9131401928" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-fdd1a7322e2a4d5faf3904f467db5028"><span style="font-size:12px">事物或情景画风不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-a861215cfe3741b197af568df6fe44f5" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-20f0b24712b44d70a72ff9ead57e0f27"><span style="font-size:12px">人体轻微引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-17c180d1df46453ab16ef75719cbfa6e">
<p id="b-dc978963bfeb4b778161fc5d93e4eafc"><span style="font-size:12px">孕肚互动类特写</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-a58ce9f6b4fe4c45a43b1517df872f0d">
<p id="b-d7f206e63f764451af99bf83626b36ea"><span style="font-size:12px">主旨特写展示正常孕妇肚子且存在互动行为，如亲吻，抚摸等。如孕肚存在妊娠纹等其他引人不适内容，按照现有标准执行。</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-4877edb4366d4c4488185e30e2c75e78">
<p id="b-4e692a8445174067905173d56d39f294"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/61937498644?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="50" height="77" id="b-9f245c937ac6468b919fea742c842776" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>

</td>

</td>

<p id="b-ecf28b72682342e1bc91dcc8aa048955"><span style="font-size:12px">引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-be4b1a8355444f989dac94708b3987d4" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-0a358a9f713247e98138c5c398c2ce48"><span style="font-size:12px">轻度令人不适</span></p>
</td>

<p id="b-37bb3c9ca75146a29865c7d7610dfd19"><span style="font-size:12px">动物轻微引人不适</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-af2c635680c64769a3f6260ad08ded59">
<p id="b-7e28118609d842f3ae9d19da80f85773"><span style="font-size:12px">器官特写</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-b16191e8f300462f979dc6c00205911f">
<p id="b-8b8cb20f35164044a2f545e064fab0c1"><span style="font-size:12px">特写展示动物生殖器，且无低俗互动。若存在低俗互动行为，则按照涉黄-软色情管控。（初审管控，高曝兜底）</span></p>
<p id="b-2a079c1bf985481f80d8321bac7bde14"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:12px">动物引人不适主要管控血腥、特写动物生殖器、恶心部位/器官等内容</span></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-2d38a2dd45944670a170d197f6a480c7">
<p id="b-63bbf9673ee34352b4e17ce327d34571"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/61938055605?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="76" height="49" id="b-91328d0f08554d238d7f170dba4bef3a" alt="image.jpeg"><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://unicorn.sankuai.com/review/generalSearch?indexType=1&amp;pkIds=686683537959&amp;dataType=0&amp;type=video&amp;pageNum=1&amp;pageSize=99&amp;search=1" title="引人不适，主旨展示动物孵化过程，存在镜头怼拍" id="b-b1b8fa8a3e464d0fa802fca714e592cb"><span style="font-size:12px">引人不适，主旨展示动物孵化过程，存在镜头怼拍</span></a></span></p>
<p id="b-b2ad542a113548beaf4d2d2ec1b07d67"><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://unicorn.sankuai.com/review/generalSearch?indexType=1&amp;pkIds=684982455888&amp;dataType=0&amp;type=video&amp;pageNum=1&amp;pageSize=99&amp;search=1" title="视频主旨拍摄动物吃奶画面，非特写动物奶头，无需按照引人不适打击，但内容低信息量，打击【视频质量差】" id="b-c36a1396b5614f1bb558764ebd99f1e5"><span style="font-size:12px">视频主旨拍摄动物吃奶画面，非特写动物奶头，无需按照引人不适打击，但内容低信息量，打击【视频质量差】</span></a></span></p>

</td>

<td id="b-90eebfb9244a4b6d998642e3aa997f1d" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">

</td>
<td id="b-588fb4ee8ba9419c8e8cd600b8f1997a" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-2b20443049c44c75bdfacc0b540c9b5f"><span style="font-size:12px">危险行为</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-b2bd27fec5df4244b80d1dcc23a1bfe1" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-063cbc3aca1f4c1c96478b4af9f52b6e"><span style="font-size:12px"><del>专业高危动作</del></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e59ffd7357ea473d9192ad7e0abc2b75" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-1642d44da6934b66b8e82e52c17c3f2b"><span style="font-size:12px">危险行为</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8825d359da894b6a8637104c730e5106" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<blockquote id="b-f419c025dbce47c28b938ef83667afe8">
<p id="b-4f4b0b16c50f41a29ab6d4dc94ffd94b"><span style="font-size:12px">管控逻辑：日常生活中一些比较容易模仿有潜在安全隐患的行为，对自己或者他人存在很大的风险性。为避免用户言目模仿，我们标注出来并进行友情提示</span></p>
</blockquote>
<p id="b-c7bab73832164d41a8d3e7d38830b291"><span style="font-size:12px">展示进食非食物类内容，予以危险行为打击（如“铅笔，玻璃，塑料等包含但不限于上述物品”）</span></p>
<ol id="b-9514a7e02a964592af9e73d19bd57c3b">
<li id="b-03256dc48bb24d3a8bb067a248708f43"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:12px">高度风险性质动作的极限运动内容，如：跑酷、冬泳（区分低危险性质的戏水）、攀岩、冲浪等等。杂技表演不控。</span></span></li>
<li id="b-61bf665f99e549758b301d454bc86c9d"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:12px">有高度风险性体育锻炼的行为</span></span></li>
<li id="b-13f19cf4d77844b28fd416e16bfa3f38"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:12px">主驾驶视角开车视频（非行驶状态不控）</span></span></li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-6ab0e53cf32941c28505713d17a6437f" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-406b68891e504b139bb3b4a8243dc911"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129501502?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="77" height="134" id="b-4084aea5ac724fe3af268dbd68d6b0fc" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129806408?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="80" height="142" id="b-f7687d2258894fe4aa0874c0b0f6ee82" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129501479?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="79" height="142" id="b-21768fcf7393470c8cffd58d9ce2bf1d" alt="image.jpeg"><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://unicorn.sankuai.com/review/generalSearch?indexType=1&amp;pkIds=685344341225,675729783967,685129236302&amp;dataType=0&amp;type=video&amp;pageNum=1&amp;pageSize=99&amp;search=1" title="打击案例" id="b-6a6f4ff9d06d491a8647498f3825d461"><span style="font-size:12px">打击案例</span></a></span><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129729159?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="93" height="162" id="b-5d8012fba3074a799c6bda380ffb898a" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53125861982?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="74" height="144" id="b-0fd3e796666c4b40950427c1ccdbd2d8" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129501412?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="73" height="130" id="b-c5eb3ef845b6454dac9ca298a616b0fe" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53126016108?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="88" height="94" id="b-82679cf90b9e485b84283e7cd4faa264" alt="原文图片（需学城权限）"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129501388?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="81" height="133" id="b-857db69a70604da8a90e978d56fb813e" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>

<p id="b-faeade0e9eaa48cd9cac647fd4a913f6"><span style="font-size:12px"><del>专业高危动作</del></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-ded45b37420d4a4ea58787371e4805e9" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-a74a3d0b84144e4a81f7e005e29b7ab8"><span style="font-size:12px">不当危险行为</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-32eaa571bbbb4a678cb75f9430f851a2" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-f322262d09cf46e1b5b17e17e0a22c86"><span style="font-size:12px">行车驾驶中的驾驶人视角的拍摄窗外或者自拍行为</span></p>
<p id="b-742cde99f0a34bfc9e3e9c64aac2808d"><span style="font-size:12px">把隧道当成拍照取景地，横穿马路、占用斑马线取景等行为</span></p>
<p id="b-1f59e9b7a11b4a4f98ed116c5ed8b41c"><span style="font-size:12px">展示、炫耀、教唆他人在非正规野泳区域进行游泳，下水玩耍行为</span></p>
</td>

<p id="b-73d7101767504006bf89dde9117c5dba"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53133814682?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="72" height="213" id="b-e85d78b6008842649894195f067e166f" alt="image.jpeg"></p>

<td id="b-3dfea59afea14601bb7a9852bf2b1e56">
<p id="b-55a5c4def8f84299b1780106cc051dd8"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-386587e9998840bdb8af4c58f9eaa691" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-4a42cd48c7eb4811bd133a9f9c4e27bd"><span style="font-size:12px">内容低质</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-4d3b256b032943dea91d1b93dea1f026" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-647c439126bd4dd4bcbed1f81d55e0fc"><span style="font-size:12px">观感低质</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-cc6f202f9f344da6a72af68103499ac5" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-9a87d6a551bc4ecb9da1e07088b6e9ee"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong><u>视频严重模糊</u></strong></span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong><u><del>/黑屏/白屏</del></u></strong></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-4b3077ebc07b488d8de038942db493b0" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-17a932eb8fe84c7b82d1637aeebc3c8a"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-829a02d512204c59b2c3d39d86e9c1f5" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-d39780567d7d4a1f995724b8de0e2254"><span style="font-size:12px">视频全程亮度低，无法辨别或大面积马赛克、视频过短（&lt;5秒）、</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">视频存在画面模糊、抖动、失真，等影响视频主旨的表达或严重影响用户观感的</span></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-43ca73cbf22149d9b93d061e9c17272b" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-8f3d0d2d2ff44cdc8730693692376131"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129501362?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="107" height="144" id="b-f3fbcd403bb649c38b2833a34c1281d3" alt="原文图片（需学城权限）"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129885981?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="93" height="215" id="b-b9e33076847f4732bf1b933313a11a9c" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53126016029?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="266" height="158" id="b-e15e5c3e45a5429b8c47e619cddbdd14" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-f3264e6f1ea845c1a8453480ef1865d3" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-49d97c354b4c46e88004d878239b991d"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-9560e55baa5147d3b24671690a676dba">
<td id="b-4dab70b032274a2c842e7f607aa2e099">
<p id="b-54ad3112b81644e0b2d7054f6bf953b4"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e2de3a4d55df417ca906fea597ed888c" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-c88bb21214b4468e95d1922136b8f76e"><span style="font-size:12px">内容低质</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e6bc195db85a47f1b7b50da659bff218" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-f22329688c8a4ee69b963f28f3ec8bf7"><span style="font-size:12px">观感低质</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-05ebaf0d538d4e269ba0fad184cb0bb9" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-c1b5ef6a3c124bfb860f6079bcf356b9"><span style="font-size:12px">内容不完整</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-80cf36798a93446c913c7f85a0ac63c4" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-e1c10fa168e44ea08f021f3fb719b293"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-1fd9c1f1ba0149b98e299345162d03d9" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<details id="b-f042f18f35e843b4bbdbeb2226677480">
<summary><strong>标准细则</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-a15ece774d2f49baa1d8a5d90653cdb8"><span style="font-size:12px">1、视频画面中主体内容（如人物、食物、字体、图片）在全篇视频被裁切不完整需打击；</span></p>
<p id="b-987ad05f13d14afdbead785b9706368c"><span style="color:rgb(0, 145, 85)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>【可放宽】内容裁剪较小不影响整体观看、以背景出现的非主体内容不全（如字母装饰等出现字母部分裁剪不全）、裁剪不全的内容一闪而过不影响内容观看</strong></span></span></p>
<p id="b-564c41fbea9b472c9de7bc9643851cf3"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>2、</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">演绎拼接影视剧人物表情内容、片段重复剪辑、劣质拼接等需按劣质拼接打击</span></p>
<p id="b-31b6a3703c564a629fe47df3966d84f4"><span style="font-size:12px">3、画面/字体等存在压缩、拉伸、旋转等扭曲形式</span></p>
<p id="b-481e37e7dd744a0790e38876688f635c"><span style="font-size:12px">4、视频中存在黑边/条/块等内容，长度或宽度或占比大于视频本身1/3及以上（即观感将近画面）同时该黑边/条/块占比整个视频的1/2及以上（即时长占比视频总长）</span></p>
<p id="b-1aa3a7537b6d4e4194b9cd8a4af6290d"><span style="font-size:12px">注：竖屏横版视频：</span></p>
<p id="b-6b98d67c3abb4efcb7a29b71a1532e1c"><span style="font-size:12px">a,</span><span style="color:rgb(0, 88, 219)"><span style="font-size:12px">非缩小或放大等正常</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">视频整体趋近于视觉中心，上下有黑边</span><span style="color:rgb(0, 88, 219)"><span style="font-size:12px">或用户自填充其他纯色，且视频主体内容≥整体的1/3</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">可豁免</span></p>
<p id="b-3d4f5d97563d47af819447573eda3cd1"><span style="font-size:12px">b,视频整体未趋近于视觉中心（即视频在边侧）如此时任意一黑边范围≥视频内容本身大小，需打击</span></p>
<p id="b-85d1d1ed22c64c24b1901e66d4cc8097"><span style="font-size:12px">c,视频本身缩小，（即上下存在黑边的同时，左右也有黑边）此时如左右黑边约占整体的1/3，则打击。</span></p>
<p id="b-ffb539c1886c4c25979ebbf619873212"><span style="font-size:12px">5、主体以非常规形式的视频（除正方形/长方形比例视频外其他所有）如圆形、三角形。【豁免：短时转场剪辑手法，若：转场形式时长占比超过视频总体时长一半及以上仍需打击】</span></p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-b6f102ed7e9a4d4b9cfb0159c1659122">
<summary><strong>参考案例解析</strong></summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-4e7923bbf72a45c585d4170a700e6118">
<li id="b-9518986c9ba54477bb58083d1cfb96b7">如图，用户自填的无任何字体或图案等的纯色（非黑色），可视作为黑边，此图可视为等比例缩小</li>
</ul>
<p id="b-e99474ba422b467896cff2b1324d8f5d"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/100158656884?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="125" height="67" id="b-c1ce0078ecfe44929b80feee76f3c587" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/100157517642?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="101" height="75" id="b-f5ba14f7fa49487b9deb440211eee19d" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/100167963738?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="128" height="92" id="b-d4bfa896fc6747b58cfda2fda1685e97" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
<p id="b-4f1014bf62bd4b2dbeb2ca21b611c509"></p>
<ul id="b-e33c29cd5f2e4bf3822a79626da96f4b">
<li id="b-f7fc043663e1444094e05efc6950fe40">如图，用户自己填充其他纯色边，但视频本身的黑边有文字/图案覆盖，可视为整体的主体，可以放宽<img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/100167167056?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="183" height="242" id="b-ffab197103fa4b999d5bd7d79a4ad854" alt="image.jpeg"></li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
</td>
<td id="b-8889cc302be44703acede7a29b942450" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<details id="b-1bcbcd4eeb5a448ea3024d713e521055">
<summary>内容不完整相关打击案例</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-b1211c6e8d464491b610a33c429d8e2a"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53132063171?contentType=1" width="97" height="214" id="b-54340b7a1da24cba8aa4905f138792ee" alt="image.jpeg"><span style="font-size:12px"> </span><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129501296?contentType=1" width="90" height="197" id="b-82deafb8e03649fd8f54c238d14bf4f6" alt="image.jpeg"><span style="font-size:12px"> </span><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53125861719?contentType=1" width="94" height="204" id="b-c9e9176362cd4f97844150f5f894d519" alt="image.jpeg"><span style="font-size:12px">  </span></p>
<p id="b-5bcf52f2ee85456fa270452ef220a33c"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53125861692?contentType=1" width="122" height="88" id="b-6502eb4d6fde44d481b56e95635be9a0" alt="原文图片（需学城权限）"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53125861688?contentType=1" width="122" height="89" id="b-9abd0fb202724a5582b0443dd3bade06" alt="原文图片（需学城权限）"><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://unicorn.sankuai.com/review/generalSearch?indexType=2&amp;pkIds=682462426343,683379251319,682313640375&amp;type=video&amp;pageNum=1&amp;pageSize=99&amp;search=1" title="视频质量差" id="b-c9b289df65ac43c0877aabab37c8b65e"><span style="font-size:12px">视频质量差</span></a></span></p>

</div>
</details>
<p id="b-048db8fd97b3475ebb5b143973951349"><a href="https://unicorn.sankuai.com/review/generalSearch?indexType=0&amp;pkIds=707998064534,708536319237,706493584447,706779004856&amp;type=video&amp;pageNum=1&amp;pageSize=99&amp;search=1" title="【打击黑边案例】" id="b-588d37868a3e4047977ca6f188178210">【打击黑边案例】</a></p>
</td>

<td id="b-25223230b2704a16a738144f4e79dbdd" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-00fd62be9a38417380efa42748a28a34"><span style="font-size:12px">低质拼凑小说</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-64cda29daf9d422c8676ee7d1bff84a9" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-17d1d73546f4492b8fb37b1b59aec3ee"><span style="font-size:12px">以字幕+口播形式，结合网络小说内容进行低成本制作，常见内容为围绕亲情、两性主题展开</span></p>
</td>

<p id="b-c64a50890f1040608b44bfc9b199ff7c"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53129501160?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="99" height="185" id="b-e4cff82b9c9f4a05a878ad58a31d38fd" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53126015808?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="95" height="181" id="b-853966f1db2f4ccdbfaeedfbbcc4f6ea" alt="image.jpeg"></p>

<p id="b-cc18337b56144611900e809041c68eb7"><span style="font-size:12px">低成本制作</span></p>

</td>
<td id="b-353fa6c4ce3947fd830097467a392230" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-b2f051ea419c4b489d6e7a3a38fc97c0"><span style="font-size:12px">低质图片二创</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-dbe1863198294e35bf01a1f6446003f1" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-2069d61c952b4e57bdd19fe7950f9ac7"><span style="font-size:12px">视频全程一张图或</span><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>同类型图</blockquote></span><span style="font-size:12px">，配有静态或动态文字，图片仅添加简单动态效果需打击（如搞笑图、诗词、资讯、心灵鸡汤，一张图讲故事，PPT轮播等）</span></p>
<p id="b-d7a817426ab341a0918d1b2904fa5369"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">ppt轮播形式可豁免：</span></span><br><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">1、热点资讯（包括明星近期动态、综艺、作品等资讯）、新闻资讯（有新闻背书）等，能够对图片进行资讯类的解说。</span></span><br><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">2、对历史照片、艺术照片有科普、教学等增量解说</span></span><br><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">3、图片连续，配有完整的故事性解说</span></span></p>
<p id="b-d88b87c4f18c4ac1a6e6d322bb3feaf8"><span style="font-size:12px">PPT轮播截取一些评论，混剪表情包/视频内容，内容连续，整体有对应的故事性解说，符合豁免标准“图片连续，配有完整的故事性解说”，可通过</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-9285c77a3a0b4a869b93db6209c76979" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<details id="b-d58353b8035d41ec9bb46e2b69013f35">

<p id="b-053cd04872504b7b80e332bc51df7834"><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://unicorn.sankuai.com/review/generalSearch?indexType=1&amp;pkIds=685111850516,684672616314&amp;dataType=0&amp;type=video&amp;pageNum=1&amp;pageSize=99&amp;search=1" title="通过案例" id="b-ea7d148aef7c4222b2512b524ea3c60a"><span style="font-size:12px">通过案例</span></a></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-165a3ada03084d1782f44a185953a4f9" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-92d3f027b6f84cf5986d3ced390f2ee7"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-11e4da59b8544ae3891f4d85e9d188c0">
<td id="b-5df503ed6417472fbd50dd82e623a526">
<p id="b-bd528c44efa54eec9a712cb401bb6661"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-fc3921f1d5c046528c1922423172ad1a" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<p id="b-5e23cf491a754a5994b313f3682d32e3"><span style="font-size:12px">内容低质</span></p>
</td>
<td rowspan="2" id="b-af79af9a69b942c69232b2d604e92b8a" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<p id="b-d280d7dcd65c49cba16f92a711452d87"><span style="font-size:12px">低成本制作</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-78275b233c784968b729eed1924b901a" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<p id="b-2fe218fa9af54295a3ab209eb3f31e5a"><span style="font-size:12px">低质随手拍</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-57c8f83276044d86a9c3a797848f0f5f" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<p id="b-aa9589d8973d45b4bec649c3c2f14569"><span style="font-size:12px">随拍随记</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-67c11bd9c0a64aba9481c60290e2b27a" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<p id="b-fc580c4edd014499b57cbb37e6e3ecdb"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>高曝，视频质量差标准原则：去低质。</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-f8efa2d143e8468188e361cf8f1fd6dc"><span style="font-size:12px">视频主体展现在各种场景下拍摄过程中没有经过精心策划和制作的内容，包括但不限于：</span></p>
<details id="b-3f15e467730c4e1e974a205ff9337566">
<summary><span style="font-size:12px">细则条款初步列举：</span></summary>
<div>
<ol id="b-7c2fd34cd3ab48a6ae4c0f4f9b06aa1f">
<li id="b-a0163299871c4b36bad1c7f97899a1aa"><span style="font-size:12px">拍摄质量较低：低质随手拍通常使用手机等便携设备进行拍摄，画面通常模糊、抖动或颜色失真。</span></li>
<li id="b-56743cc97b1e4646a4e05a84e32d13fa"><span style="font-size:12px">视频画面主旨内容与标题无任何关联或关联度弱（牵强度高）。</span></li>
<li id="b-9fed3e38dcb74c438123fe858ff78ed7"><span style="font-size:12px">视频大字报文案与视频画面无关或弱关联需打击。例如心灵鸡汤配风景照</span></li>
<li id="b-0db4a381eb3f4df699492a2f08d81799">
<p id="b-e1706433b5fd46ba944f0611c197de8d"><span style="font-size:12px">缺乏剪辑和后期处理：低质随手拍往往没有经过剪辑和后期处理，原始拍摄内容直接发布。这使视频可能存在拍摄中的错误、镜头切换不流畅等问题。【豁免：内容有意义且标题文字部分对视频内容有说明的随手拍、风景优美画质清晰且标题有文案解说、街拍时尚达人、或才艺技巧展示类豁免、展现萌宠生活习性、或萌宠萌娃搞笑视频豁免】主旨展示萌宠可豁免通过，萌宠不限定家养宠物，拍摄动物园、野生等场景可爱动物也适当放宽。</span></p>
<p id="b-2fb2390b2d1445f0a965f5103e77cf8b"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>※</strong></span></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">【所有豁免项审核逻辑均需判断是否有主题，演绎成本等，在判断是否为萌娃。如</span></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://s3plus.sankuai.com/save-video/6anZk1wNhmSS0GSpCghAquP-keE0cF9rP5Zm2aKCiQI=_video_compressed.mp4" title="该视频（小孩不洗草莓直接吃）" id="b-9ace190960da4b7baf10bbaae1ca6eb9"><span style="color:rgb(22, 111, 247)"><span style="font-size:12px">该视频（小孩不洗草莓直接吃）</span></span></a></span></span><span style="color:rgb(22, 111, 247)"><span style="font-size:12px">、</span></span><span style="color:rgb(22, 111, 247)"><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://s3plus.sankuai.com/save-video/cTxmK8JaoXJSz4HoQXVsG3ducHdWUc2lCozIlnhzsY0=_video_compressed.mp4" title="该视频（父亲陪孩子玩沙子）" id="b-2fd75ebfdee640908a038dabba66a30b"><span style="color:rgb(22, 111, 247)"><span style="font-size:12px">该视频（父亲陪孩子玩沙子）</span></span></a></span></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">虽然小孩非认知高颜值的萌娃，但整体有演绎主题在，仍可豁免。】</span></span></p>
</li>
<li id="b-66f3f7bf84814cd1a21d0f0f09c0e2c1"><span style="font-size:12px">内容简单随意：低质随手拍通常是片段短小、内容简单的视频</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">（无相关解说）</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">。如：日常生活中的瞬间、随手拍摄的非展现颜值自/摆拍等，视频无主题或通过视频周边仍无法明确主题的。</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">【豁免：</span></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong><u>非负向的</u></strong></span></span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">土味演绎类、低智演绎类、有笑点、泪点、共鸣、内容增量的视频】</span></span></li>
<li id="b-86af7cfc5fb040f09a6ad4f7b1746c42">
<p id="b-dc2d53db5dbe4080b4e7c47f11476d46"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">无内容增量：蜡</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">像相关内容，如是随手拍的</span><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>非名人</strong></span><span style="font-size:12px">蜡像或者蜡像局部，且结合标题和文案、无其他内容增量的，单纯的蜡像随拍</span></p>
<ol id="b-23f5f13979b249aa99b2769492dc63fd">
<li id="b-4fcbeaef2d634cc89236fe81e5e25482">
<p id="b-825f5a056e014fb8983abd82bd3511a6"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>随拍内容增量定义阐述：</strong></span></span></p>
<ol id="b-55e142ab65a04631842c3adf36d6fd92">
<li id="b-1f24d43c40344bbd99820c2d210fa700"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">视频画面内容呈现本身就比较丰富：即非单场景直拍，内容有主旨表达、存在一定有剪辑、运镜、脚本等制作成本</span></span></li>
<li id="b-5d4e62fdb5524a3c8a045e862670d833"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">视频画面内容简单，但标题、文案、口播等对视频有对应细节说明：包含但不限于内容背景介绍、原因分析、细节拆分、讲解说明、教授行为等信息的增加</span></span></li>
<li id="b-63e683c4f1f84cfc834c0f2707ccbaad"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">内容标题、文案无详细介绍，无内容增量</span></span></li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-922686ed3bbb4be2a56d9290113764ad"><span style="font-size:12px">豁免：蜡像制作过程、蜡像馆参观展览、名人蜡像且内容里有讲述人物历史等无任何引人不适的</span></p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-f14c269d426b49baa90946acd207c7df">
<summary><span style="font-size:12px">重点关注场景列举：</span></summary>
<div>
<ol id="b-cfeb6b69993746b5a79d88b7bbc0aa88">
<li id="b-8c0b546e479a4e60872cd26265daf448"><span style="font-size:12px">日常生活中的趣事：如街头表演、路边奇闻等。</span></li>
<li id="b-c2854f43f3604841ae3df988fd268393"><span style="font-size:12px">搞笑短片：如恶搞、恶作剧等。</span></li>
<li id="b-c3ffed55fc9b43e19c13cdf80642b6df"><span style="font-size:12px">节日庆典活动：如婚礼、生日派对等。</span></li>
<li id="b-f1292ae42f23475585497715b7e78751"><span style="font-size:12px">旅行和探险：如拍摄旅行中的景点、美食等。</span></li>
<li id="b-7af9ab2886fe4376989223dc3bb7aae1"><span style="font-size:12px">自拍和自述：如分享个人经历、抒发情感等。</span></li>
<li id="b-5a4dff90b3e8478fa3c1dd51918b5c94"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">动画：单一游戏录屏画面无解说、无剪辑等增量内容需打击</span></span></li>
</ol>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-dfacc16a8d6040a6ada9b42a3de5059c">
<summary><span style="font-size:12px">注意</span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-d34913cd17d0495d9ef2eb710013372d"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">随手拍：指不经过专业摄影设备准备，用手中的手机或其他便携式数码相机等设备随时随地拍摄的照片或视频。通常以即兴的、不经过特别构思或准备的，主要目的是捕捉当下的瞬间或记录自己身边的事物、人物或景观。相对较为随意性和即兴性，与专业摄影作品相比，技术、构图和后期处理可能较为简单或粗糙。</span></span></p>
<p id="b-99f47317f4074ab085fa3195cbf83e7a"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">关联度：视频与标题有关联指的是视频的内容与标题所描述的主题或内容相符合，能够准确地概括视频的主题或内容。关联度较弱则指视频的内容与标题不是非常相关或者不能够准确地概括视频的主题或内容。</span></span></p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-43013eb86e254a3c8d32644aa600df98">
<summary><span style="font-size:12px">标准细节明确：</span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-be17b920ea034f27b94b85af30abda83"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">1、颜值类需要露脸</span></span><br><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">2、萌宠萌娃需要是视频主旨</span></span></p>
<p id="b-74c36a2a343648048418dfd1f427b7f7"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">3、舞蹈动作、健身锻炼教授，有步骤介绍，非单一动作内容无需打击；内容简单，单一动作，无教授行为，无场景转换需打击</span></span></p>
<p id="b-312f8f048dc14079a128158aceaefdda"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">4、</span></span><span style="color:rgb(212, 30, 33)"><span style="font-size:12px">负面情绪演绎：此类内容把控婆媳冲突、家暴等家庭关系矛盾，豁免有正向教育意义内容；如负面情绪中的婆媳关系演绎，且结局没有和好、悔改、正能量输出的内容需要打击；如果是为了通过矛盾演绎进行正能量的传递不需要打压</span></span></p>
<ul id="b-6b76f3dcace34faab1a10814f9058e4e">
<li id="b-92a59057a6af456b9bebbde4696ceb8b"><span style="color:rgb(212, 30, 33)"><span style="font-size:12px">负面情绪列举场景：负面情绪指的是一种消极的情绪状态，通常包括愤怒、恐惧、焦虑、沮丧、悲伤等不良情绪假如是民生伤亡资讯，传递的也是负面情绪，但是不需要打压；短视频要打压的是传递负能量，给网友传递讲述生活不幸、婚姻不幸、心理疾病、抑郁、社交矛盾之类且没有正向引导的内容（包括自述场景、演绎场景等）</span></span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-d27b5b028e094cf29fea4c97ef023389"><span style="font-size:12px">5、特效内容，有明确故事剧情或多片段间有一定的衔接性内容可通过</span></p>
<p id="b-91ac556d982642c4b55f56040054e9e6"><span style="font-size:12px">6、美食展示，艺术品，编织，手工艺品等特写镜头展示，画面干净、饱和度高可通过</span></p>
</div>
</details>
</td>
<td id="b-ea5b330a2b074c3d88f075379e99e967" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<details id="b-1d6b4a7e1d5045b5a575d2fd1e983d09">

<td id="b-8b8d342d3b184a9e80f88d834f2b1cce" style="background-color:#E8F1FF">
<details id="b-ccf057c6c7db4ef8a144f2fefa4ec591">
<summary><span style="font-size:12px">参考案例</span></summary>
<div>
<table id="b-21a6a262533f44049ae24707723fe743">
<tr id="b-aee6bbfa8d4f473180424228f42a612f">
<td id="b-98764359a8cb4d41b41a26d8ae152c20" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-2e958e98262945a08e6fed904ac91c18"><span style="font-size:12px">舞蹈：</span><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://unicorn.sankuai.com/review/generalSearch?indexType=0&amp;dataType=0&amp;pkIds=680417118873&amp;type=video&amp;pageNum=1&amp;pageSize=99&amp;search=1" title="（正例，无需打击）" id="b-5706c19304294c17a947bb14bc178e05"><span style="font-size:12px">（正例，无需打击）</span></a></span></p>
<p id="b-c09d48add4b84066bec903ba1dae02a3"><span style="font-size:12px">街拍&amp;颜值：</span></p>
<ul id="b-0debe792f0ef4eba9ba7f05177e4c735">

<p id="b-3889d3fc7d81465cb5222e29f55e4258"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">2）无关拼接演绎：视频文案讲述一段感性故事，但拍摄内容前后镜头无关联或关联性差需打击。</span></span><br><span style="font-size:12px">豁免：案件类，民间故事（不涉及危言耸听，谣传等），历史故事类等，穿插相关的影视剧镜头，讲述的是完整故事</span></p>

</td>
<td id="b-9cb67f160a224a468ec1beaee68c77b7" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-aa09f537223c40cf948ed5d4bca21b58"><span style="font-size:12px">流量造假</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-106dc0383c304e4f9d88f71823b64cda" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-6b31b3f43bbc499690604bac0bbfcd3c"><span style="font-size:12px">诱骗交互</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-924c99bcaf8340bb9d29325226d0c41b" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-3832e19cd72848ceb6e45a2856fbb315"><span style="font-size:12px">主观骗互动</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-ace23cb97c5a40808a989cfcb439da20" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-6c740983fe89428d819ce6beceaff112"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-5f8bccfb1d34440f8cb5149d27413fea" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-f881e2453e744e5d8774ebba17148e4b"></p>
<p id="b-d19004af26f245d9969a042131ce83dc"><span style="font-size:12px">1）视频通过悬念类表述，以欺骗，诱导为手段，达到骗赞，骗关注的目的，需打击</span></p>
<p id="b-b6b7039584f44c4183a36092615ae750"><span style="font-size:12px">如：双击屏幕有惊喜；点两下可放大图片；点两下有XX；</span></p>
<p id="b-6c6e46c00a9e4e9d8be94e7fe3b45464"><span style="font-size:12px">2）明确表达点赞/关注会获益需打击</span></p>
<p id="b-ba7477dbe909435f955a128038f2ec4c"><span style="font-size:12px">如：点赞瘦十斤、点赞行大运；点赞接财神；为我点赞，我就可以XXX</span></p>
<p id="b-f4aa8205b08a4adfa36c405206a4203e"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">3）引导互动：视频内容附带大字文案，且引导用户互动点赞评论、送祝福、留足迹等行为。</span></span></p>
<p id="b-6275eb8e8c0a49f7934bb76453fce510"><span style="font-size:12px">4）视频单场景直拍，无镜头切换，文字采用大字报形式，图文弱关联，利用同理心，道德绑架等行为要求点赞，关注需打击</span></p>
<p id="b-dd5b0b7a87f84eb2b4703026bf957001"><span style="font-size:12px">如：孩子双亲去世，点赞鼓励孩子成长；老人孤苦，点赞支持</span></p>
<p id="b-868aafc2b9314a1e9eab180109114ebb"><span style="font-size:12px">豁免：</span></p>
<p id="b-1da185cccf344c4ca827c3deaef53936"><span style="font-size:12px">真人出镜，视频文字与视频画面强关联，基于视频内容客观引导点赞可通过（单一画面不视为强关联）</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-af969b858fc84ad8b94036ec240d0e76" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-325cace1fbca4c578375f7ac1288d75f"><span style="font-size:12px">定义1）对应案例：</span></p>
<p id="b-7a251f965ea7456c97d085236f273909"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53133018989?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="84" height="141" id="b-8b406cf98bcd45efa31ff47d82201e89" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53133018935?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="81" height="125" id="b-25fa7b583e3d4324b66d43cc7c1ef79d" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53132220472?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="20" height="34" id="b-475e738b82524545a851742df5f9d6a9" alt="image.jpeg"></p>

<p id="b-0fb347618ffe4b41a032060052d552ff"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53131901471?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false" width="67" height="112" id="b-bd59fd2540234cf0a73cd9483ed49d01" alt="image.jpeg"></p>

</div>
</details>
<p id="b-1e2211857dee454b926ce9b777d5f58b"><span style="font-size:12px">4）对应案例：</span></p>
<p id="b-dc60013638ad4238baf7fe6ef06c9570"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53131901720?contentType=1" width="117" height="169" id="b-39949b0e7c754ba1bf754bc0c37afaf1" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53126015324?contentType=1" width="107" height="184" id="b-3be9b50625934de4835c82327369b944" alt="image.jpeg"><img src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/1678985687/53125861082?contentType=1" width="116" height="188" id="b-f607aa9ad8e84a73bfc6d841f62dc9fc" alt="image.jpeg"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-f26aa12555fb4f95aee426a9d763f0ca" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">

</td>

<tr id="b-8cf6678dae024ce9ae1faf8928e02ae0">
<td id="b-2048cae524114dc0bea12df3e1ec2e8f">
<p id="b-e5427b8ae8564932b14a6979059f13c2"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8428721e2f4f40b9b355aa770ad222e9" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-2bf9d2a8049e46cdbec1501acfceb57c"><span style="font-size:12px">内容低质</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-d6fb2c37b0db47f981f8b54c4f159533" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-d3e91f1cec9843338754eb4cfb330a11"><span style="font-size:12px">过时效</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-84312227ce134775b2cae3fd194dc986" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-c0e9efa30e8f437ba692fee173265d6b"><span style="font-size:12px">-</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-0727082927624a819d014b85c0b0c93e" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-b6489818ba9c4787bebeac83d2ce7d26"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8be85978436d48f38cbe7eccdb64561f" style="background-color:rgb(237, 250, 244)">
<p id="b-310ddbcb8a6943f3a0fb7455d5248957"><span style="font-size:12px">事件本身具有时效性，可根据时间推导出内容</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>剩余可消费时间</strong></span></span><span style="font-size:12px">，高曝审核时间离视频中明确提及或暗示的时间</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>超过</strong></span></span><span style="font-size:12px">消费时效需打击。</span></p>
<ol id="b-9f23490e83254ba6993bb20c1da17bc0">
<li id="b-c26d1b62008c4f11be3c068237272522"><span style="font-size:12px">有具体活动时间点。如时装周、电影节、国庆节、新品上市、开幕等7天</span></li>
<li id="b-52f7b598201a4d0db52656f3dfa7f2b9"><span style="font-size:12px">民生趣闻/资讯内容时效要求为3天</span></li>
<li id="b-604d9f22f84a46f0858eeba97f40dc78"><span style="font-size:12px">视频中提到将来的时间是过去已发生的事件，直接按照过时效打击。如：（1.18是腊八，1.23审到视频，视频内容为：明天是腊八节）</span></li>
</ol>
<details id="b-399bef3fb77b40edba985eb367233428">
<summary><span style="font-size:12px">娱乐资讯相关的时效要求为7天</span></summary>
<div>
<ol id="b-3f9ba62ee7b242f8aecfc7756dd267fc">
<li id="b-e3de857bba4e46d39d03ea1dc80d9280"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>一般娱乐新闻</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">：非聚焦突发事件的明星八卦、明星活动资讯等</span></li>
<li id="b-00d3a4645e1741ca9d108905f1c31f10"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>影视资讯</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">：电影、电视、综艺节目、动漫动画上映/下映/开播/完播，播出过程中的趣事等相关资讯以及明星歌手演出资讯、音乐类资讯等等</span></li>
<li id="b-fdb98bc54b6947d89ae90cc1d6031ef3"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>科技资讯</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">：最新科技研发进展</span></li>
<li id="b-28c534267aa04fcb8f83afb3f0c04c48"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>明星娱评类内容：</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">无论视频中是否有明确日期标识或近日近期等表述，凡是围绕明星的个人生活、影视作品中的表现、综艺表现等进行点评的内容，均标为短时效</span></li>
<li id="b-708edf0b81384bada25328c81e1ec6a1"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>表演、展览、活动资讯类内容</span></span></li>
<li id="b-3a8e99ba4808435bb5fa413de8eb766f"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>游戏资讯</span></span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">：游戏领域相关资讯，比如游戏上下线、版本更新以及与主播、官方等相关的资讯类信息</span></span></li>
<li id="b-df6e97c86beb4cdc9ea398577bcb202d"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>人物资讯</span></span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">：围绕各行各业名人展开的资讯类内容</span></span></li>
<li id="b-adbe26aa75a042b6b0192ab0beeca699"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><span>预告类内容</span></span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">：如</span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>未来一周内的</strong></span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">预告类内容（天气预告、赛事预告、追剧预告、开播预告、上映预告、开票预告、直播预告等等）</span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>【3天、4天、5天、6天、一周】上映倒计时</strong></span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">、</span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>直播预告</strong></span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">等；</span></span></li>
</ol>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-9d8ef24dbcd5466b858ddecd67a84e77"><span style="font-size:12px">如视频中，无任何时间节点的明/暗示，且整体无任何其他违规，则不按照过时效去打击。</span></p>
<p id="b-f77b64b1d6ba49a8a4c130db6bb5de8f"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">资讯事件衍生出的英雄故事、事迹，好人好事等，可算长时效；如：视频提及 2022年3月某地暴雪导致雪灾，某人舍小家利大家，视频主旨弘扬该行为。虽然事件为多年前，但仍然可以继续消费，此类不打击过时效。</span></span></p>
</td>

<td id="b-1bc52ad12feb4801a420f2e3639365a8" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<p id="b-d81bd4eed9f84ff091e4087b5e777637"><span style="font-size:12px">以任一品类为主体，</span></p>
<p id="b-a1c5e295aacb4a1eaa60777f906c607d"><span style="font-size:12px">内容无作者本人的使用体验和感受，只有尺码/材质罗列（时尚类单品）、产品功效的官方宣传词（美妆护肤）、成分和功效的罗列（保健品）等推广词需打击。</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">针对有一定体验但整体依然是植入内容的仍需打击</span></span></p>
<p id="b-9fc81f1d5aa24773aa83c6c397fc10d5"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">*不限于是否出现产品名，一律打击</span></span></p>
<p id="b-843940b8aaf84751a4ab70fe1957cfd5"><span style="font-size:12px">多个产品测评（或探店）推荐且有作者本人真实体验内容可通过。</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-3b59e30d812040c2b7af70e349dc79ba" style="background-color:rgb(232, 241, 255)">
<details id="b-467801cdf035488ebb7aa150a6a749fa">

<p id="b-4066eb70abb24fd68eced7af889f7c8e"><span style="font-size:12px">性话题</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-adf111bd773a450ab5bb53de78832b16" style="background-color:#EDFAF4">
<p id="b-cbe9a3afb9254f4b9d9357bff167a935"><span style="font-size:12px">涉性不良价值资讯</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-7f7e07c5438843649208bd6f01540631" style="background-color:#EDFAF4">
<p id="b-acc2b940e92d4b7a95965960a46b4a66"><span style="font-size:12px">资讯类内容主体为性侵、乱伦、感情不忠（出轨、偷情、捉奸等）、性交易、性相关工作者、潜规则</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">、</span></span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:12px">或极易引起争议性涉黄类的内容，</span></span><span style="font-size:12px">且视频画面出现疑似违规动作或画面引起噱头需打击。</span></p>
<p id="b-9e759f1a02d840aca730c0a39846c30a"><span style="color:#000"><span style="font-size:12px">视频/标题内容存在非科学法律科普，主旨博眼球，如利用“40w一晚”“女子轮奸男性”等低俗用词</span></span></p>
<p id="b-5d324220502648e49f517d2cdec30684"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:12px">强奸、奸杀等离奇死亡、擦边死亡案件。</span></span></p>
</td>

<p id="b-e1899dcaa7544413bfba8cbf3a02be08"><span style="font-size:12px"><blockquote>医疗</blockquote></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c44c516c1f784177906d3b50ddc32d3f">
<p id="b-9bfe0faa9dfb402e98dfce645d9712ed"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong><blockquote>无资质医疗</blockquote></strong></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c476bf12f26541d2b6b37f44e199e158">
<p id="b-25d520f2d9794614b54c24de57a2cb70"><span style="font-size:12px">视频主旨展示需具备专业资质才可科普的专业医疗内容，具体：</span></p>
<p id="b-c6f423e272c44786826d7a5a933c9456"><span style="font-size:12px">1、包含详细症状诊断以及治疗方案推荐，如手术、激光治疗等</span></p>
<p id="b-0dfbd50f47be45ddbab026166313ab95"><span style="font-size:12px">2、包含具体药品名称、药品图推荐、治疗药效说明、医疗器械等，</span></p>
<p id="b-eec15baee4204497ab48a6fcacda7b44"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>豁免：药食同材类如冬虫夏草</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-59410e8a7a454a8b88480ee5ddb5ba27"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong>（初审管控，复审兜底）</strong></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-403bfcaf286d40d59336a9c4de68b57e">
<p id="b-58c1b2e5b9ad4c2dba014c979b0c3cc7"><span style="font-size:12px">1、治疗方案</span></p>

<p id="b-a17fae49274f4673ae3ab0d679f8d16a"><span style="font-size:12px">4、</span><span style="font-size:12px"><a href="https://s3plus.sankuai.com/save-video/2LRCPYqNzjWIcRa33HNwNHVTzZsYUuSBKDHepcn1sZI=_video_compressed.mp4" title="无科普性质，纯展示药品" id="b-f060d16b79c844f0b88cd9ccf2d5c24a"><span style="font-size:12px">无科普性质，纯展示药品</span></a></span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-567cec5f8502471ea2fb442cfba3316b">

<td id="b-fc06bcfab85a479cafad84cd1eaf177e">

</td>
<td id="b-bcc55a7642bb45d28b0c80ffa31eae93">
<p id="b-423f9b9fea4f4bcfb15c4a13d2c5b126"><span style="font-size:12px">无备案短剧</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-5131d24eaced47ceb0b9e044ae95d8d5">
<p id="b-589be4879ff24292957bfb3631a73288"><span style="font-size:12px">视频为短剧形式且有明显剧集特征，一般单集时长在几分钟左右，传达一个完整的故事情节或者展现一个特定的事件情境，一般有明确上下集引导观看的微型连续剧；</span></p>
<p id="b-de4f6f937d0b4200a7ad940034ccb8c3"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:12px">包括微短剧二创内容，如剪辑解说类</span></span></p>
<p id="b-ca857bfc66a04b6d80c58c018a06f64c"><span style="color:#E0E0E0"><span style="font-size:12px"><strong><del>豁免：有备案号短剧</del></strong></span></span></p>
<details id="b-022fed87cbe54e24bf7db0f5cb743af4">
<summary>新标准</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-54078a1139c14357b5be31466add7c26"><span style="font-size:15px">打标：</span></p>
<ol id="b-43f6d5ddba334f1bbc02457554f052fe">
<li id="b-70162654ea714d8cb56589e53a8b3d19">
<p id="b-3ecd3e6305ab4608915188a5c473cb53"><span style="font-size:15px">视频为短剧形式，或者有明显剧集特征的微短剧。或者有明确上下集引导观看的微型电视剧，微短剧的解说视频。</span></p>

<li id="b-5767b1889b014fcd9eb22766b964cf7d"><span style="font-size:15px">有备案号的，但备案号是新版备案号但非美团备案号的，如  【（快手/抖音等等）网微剧备字（2024）第002号】</span></li>
</ol>

</ol>

<li id="b-73887da1bcb24806b2bdafd1c133f00f"><span style="font-size:12px">电影和电视剧恶搞：视频主体对知名电影、电视剧进行恶搞模仿，通过夸张的表演和改编情节来制造恶俗笑料；如命中现有规则则按照现有规则执行；</span></li>
<li id="b-418895247a204f2ea468eaf1e3c575f3"><span style="font-size:12px">名人模仿：模仿名人的言行举止、表情动作等，通过夸张和讽刺来制造低俗娱乐效果。</span></li>
<li id="b-e9ffdfc9fcb343ef89073ea542321fcb"><span style="font-size:12px">幽默综艺节目：视频内容为部分综艺节目剪辑，如特定环节或角色专门的恶俗演绎</span></li>
<li id="b-ae19d873848b47de86e6919cfedaad4e"><span style="font-size:12px">网络恶搞视频：通过视频剪辑、模仿等手法，对网络热点、流行文化进行恶搞和讽刺。</span></li>
</ol>
</td>
